tbmm-2020-12-25sit02spe0111par0148	Thank you, Mr. President. Mr. President, dear lawmakers, I'm going to share with you the views of our group's Democratic Party on the entire Law Proposal Against the Financing of Public Disposals, first and foremost, I salute you all. I'm going to make my assessment in two chapters: I'm going to try to sum up our country's FATF accession process in the first chapter, whether or not our country is being obeyed in this process, if our country is being subjected to any sanctions, whether we can be prevented from entering the gray list by the FATF; and in the second chapter, I'm going to try to make a summary of whether this arrangement meets FATF Yes, the FATF, the Financial Action Task Force, was founded within the OECD, but independently by G7 countries in 1989. In the beginning, the aim of the organisation was to fight the laundering of black money, to make it a standard for the legislation and banking system of countries. Turkey also became a member of the FATF (FATF) in 1991. Turkey carried out its first legal arrangement 15 years later in 2006, with the Law on the Absolving Crime revenues No. 5549. FATF, meanwhile, added to FATF's mandate to fight terrorism financing at its general council in 2001. Exactly 12 years later, Turkey carried out an arrangement with the Law on the Protection of Terrorism No. 6415 in 2013. There are 40 basic recommendations for implementation by FATF member states, and nine special advice decisions have been made. The FATF monitors periodically the development and compliance of member states with these decisions and international contracts, reporting developments. Turkey was subjected to a thorough examination of the general council in September 2009. During the study process, a plan of action was presented to Turkey at the general board meeting held in February 2010, and Turkey was listed as gray, and Iran and North Korea were on the black list. The FATF said that Turkey's membership would be suspended if it did not take the necessary steps by February 2013, and that Turkey would be blacklisted along with Iran and North Korea. In February 2013, the Law on the Financing of Terrorism No. 6415 came into effect, and Turkey was saved from the blacklist and then removed from the gray list. This is the third time we've been through this. FATF conducted a 14-month survey of Turkey, a two-week field study during which time, meeting many in Turkey. A 238-page report was published in 2019 as a result of field work. I call it published, but according to the Financial Times, in fact, Turkey has been lobbying even to prevent this report from being published, but that's not the topic of discussion here; anyway... In fact, it's a law proposal, one of which is temporary, one of which should be made up of nine articles, one of which should be executive, one of which should be in effect. As for the evaluations of the substances, I'd like to first say a few words about the 20th article of the law's obligation to keep secrets. If this offer is not withdrawn, the audit staffs assigned by the Ministry of Internal Affairs and the Ministry of Interior will be able to ask lawyers for any information, documents, and records in all kinds of environments, including the move-in sales, the establishment of the company, the foundation and association, and the administration and the transfer of the law. It is possible to say that this arrangement will open a door to eliminate the obligation of lawyers to keep secrets and the obligation to keep secrets, which is hundreds or thousands of years old. When the proposal was discussed by the Justice Commission, the bidders said: Michaud/Fransa's decision had an application to the European Court of Human Rights, and the court did not find it contrary to the European Convention on Human Rights. And since we didn't have the decision at the time, we probably did. But then we learned that the practice in France is not about the Ministry of Internal Affairs or any public authority, but about the Bar Association, where the lawyer is only a member of it. The lawyer will be able to provide information to the Bar and Bar Association, which he is a member of, but in Turkey, all of this information can be examined by the Ministry of Interior and auditers. tbmm-2020-12-25sit02spe0207par0560	Thank you, Mr. President. First of all, I'd like to thank our lawmakers for asking questions. Mr. Aycan, Mr. Gaytancıoğlu, Mr. Bakırlıoğlu, Mr. Tanal, Mr. Gündoğdu, Mr. Simsek, Mr. Erdem and Mr. Sevkin will be referred to the ministries regarding the questions. When we came to the answers to the questions of our lawmakers asking about the proposal, Mr. Karahocagil asked if there were regulations in the proposal that narrowed down the freedom of the association, such as membership in organisations, and taking office in governing bodies? To sum up the amendments to the Associations' Law here: Article 1 is included in the 1st article, as well as in the associations and foundations located abroad, which is not a violation of the freedom to establish an association. The amendment to the right to establish an association law shall not serve as a member of the association board, for those convicted of financing terrorism, drug trafficking, laundering the value of criminal property, there is such a limit. This, of course, is one of the recommendations of the International Financial Action Force. There are regulations concerning the audit, the audit of Article 19 of the Associations Act, the audit of public officials, and its fees, its powers. And these are not regulations that provide a limitation on the freedom of the association. There used to be a notification of foreign aid from abroad about the aid of the associations, and there is also a duty to inform on the aid of the new arrangement abroad. Again, there is no arrangement here that frees you from the freedom of association. The heavily debated Article 30/A is added to the Act of Associations. Again, there are three crimes related to a person I just mentioned, who can only be authorized by the Interior Minister at a temporary precautionary measure, and if this measure is not enough in haste, the Interior Minister will be able to temporarily halt the association's activity, but will immediately appeal to the court, the judge will need approval, and within 48 hours, the judge's decision will be made without delay. If the institution's governing body has been relieved of duty by the judge's decision, it is also stated in the relevant article of our Civil Law, Article 27, how to make a trustee appointment. Again, there is no restrictions on the freedom to form an association. Criminal provisions in the Law of Associations are being updated again, adapting to the recommendations of the International Financial Action Force (FATF). There is no limiting this right to freedom of association. Turkey has been a country that has been fighting different terrorist organisations and different forms of terrorism for years, Mr. Taşkın said. He said that we believe in the necessity of international cooperation in the fight against the financing of terrorism, and we agree with that. What arrangements are there in the proposal for the implementation of such decisions by the United Nations Security Council? When we look at the proposal of the law, legislation is under way to implement the UN Security Council's decisions to prevent financing weapons of mass destruction. In this way, it contributes to preventing the spread of weapons of mass destruction and international security. With the law, a audit and cooperation commission is being established, the established audit and cooperation commission is aimed at providing an effective co-direction in implementing decisions. In this framework, sanctions are determined to provide funds for individuals and organisations, supply and equipment trade, technology transfer banning, and procedures for freezing property. Mr. Durmişoglu asked if there was any restrictions on the fundraising of the Associations. He asked about the purpose of the arrangements for the Gathering Law. The proposal briefly sums up the arrangements for the Gathering Law: A amendment is being made to the 6th Amendment of the Law for Help, and a permit is already required. Associations that work for the public benefit are already an exception, including around 28 associations and foundations. What's the arrangement? The online publication on the release of content if it has been detected in the Internet environment is being arranged for the removal of that content. There is also a provision for the study of the application in Article 9 of the Gathering Law. There was no opening in our current legislation on domestic and foreign aid. There's an arrangement for the regulation to be taken out. There is an arrangement related to the audit of the fundraising operation, about those assigned, that makes it more effective for the collection of information and documents related to this audit from the authorities concerned with their powers. Again, there is an update on the penalties in the Gathering Law. A sanction is being brought in line with the recommendations of the Financial Action Task Force. There's no hesitation in that. Attorneys are included in the Article 2 of the Law on the Absolving of Criminal Influence of Attorneys of Mr. Kılıç. He asked: "Is there a situation here that has a negative effect on the right to defend? " Again, there is a change in the definition of the Law on the Absorption of Criminal Influence, and lawyers are added here. Currently operating in the field of mail and transport, good fortune and betting games, through other financial services and other financial services; exchange, non-moving, valuable stones and minerals, jewelry transport, business machine, historical artifacts and antique trades, noters and sports clubs are now under the obligation of lawyers, but there are limits to this report. Lawyers are required to be included in terms of their right to defend themselves, not in violation of other laws. Except for the first joke of Article 35 of the Law of Law. Lawyers are entitled to do all the procedures, not just in the courts, but in the state offices. Here, as part of the Law on the Absolving Crime Influence of revenues, the suspicious transaction notification is only limited by the management of the assets in these accounts, including the move-instruction, the establishment of the same rights, the removal, the merger of the company or the foundation, and the implementation of financial transactions related to their management, transfer and liquidation. Therefore, there is no regulation in Article 35 of the Law that would undermine the right to defend itself and other laws. It is also part of FATF's recommendation to include lawyers, and many of the member states have made such arrangements.

Our country has worked so hard so far in peace and peace, for a safe world, to cooperate with the international community, to prevent the spread of terrorism and weapons of mass destruction, and so on. Our country's strategic position always requires a more effective fight against terrorist organizations. In particular, we need international co-operation to succeed in the fight against the PKK and many other terrorist organizations over the years, and financing and cutting off human resources to eliminate terrorism are mandatory. To date, different regulations have been made in our country as part of the fight against financing weapons of mass destruction based on UN Security Council decisions. Today, however, increasing international risks regarding weapons of mass destruction have led to a need for new regulations involving more effective, more deterrent sanctions in international co-operation. The Financial Action Task Force has also become a member of our country, inviting UN member states of the United Nations Security Council to comply with international standards developed by the FATF, and making recommendations through mutual assessment of member states. An assessment was made with our country in October 2019, at the end of this evaluation process, emphasising the importance of strengthening legal and institutional capacity in dealing with UN Security Council decisions and FATF recommendations to combat terrorism financing and laundering crimes. The weapons of mass destruction are nuclear, chemical, and biological, and can do great damage to life, property, and environment. To prevent that, you must first stop spreading, eliminate your financing. The Law Proposal 247 is a proposal aimed at adapting to international standards, which further improves the existing legislation in our country to carry out this goal. The proposal prohibits action and activities forbidden to prevent the implementation of the UN Security Council's decisions on the issue, actions and activities forbidden to prevent the spread of weapons of mass destruction, banning and preventing collection of funds against decisions made by the United Nations Security Council, opening representations of individuals and organisations on international lists of banned actions, operating, becoming business partners, accessing to capital partnerships, establishing ties with banks. The decisions of the United Nations Security Council to prevent the spread of weapons of mass destruction are being organised in the way and the basis for freezing the assets of individuals and organisations. Again, the audit and cooperation commission is established to ensure the purpose of the law. Again, judicial and administrative sanctions are being carried out regarding those who are in violation of the legal arrangement, who are failing to comply with their decision to freeze property or who are being neglected, who are delaying. Again, in non-public corporations, it is a key risk to the financing of terrorism, that the transfer of state-of-the-art forms to the bearership of non-public companies can only be carried out through the handover of zigzag. For this, changes are required in the Turkish Trade Law. In addition, provisions aimed at increasing controls and more effective implementation of administrative sanctions in order to prevent the financing of terrorism and the spread of weapons of mass destruction are being made by amendments to the Law of Aid No. 2860 and Associations No. 5253. Our talks with the Justice Commission have brought some criticism of the proposal, but the security of our country and the entire world is inevitable to be held accountable for certain obligations in the face of the superior benefits of society. It is also important for our international obligation to adopt this law in the face of the advice of international institutions in which the world is now entering a digital age, where financing of terrorism and weapons of mass destruction has become much more difficult in this digital age, where effective regulations have been made in all developed countries, and in which our country is a member. With these thoughts, I salute the General Assembly.

The question is, is there any nuclear fuel in the world that is much more peaceful, much safer, and impossible to produce weapons of mass destruction? Congressmans, this element is in Turkey. So, what's the name of this element? Thorium. Turkey is number four in the world with 400,000 tons of thorium reserves. So, how do you get energy from thorium? I'll tell you this: the plasma-shaped hydrogen proton is ejected inside a 50-metre proton accelerator, its core hits a heavy metal, the core is split, and the neutron cluster reacts with thorium-232, and emerges thorium-233. It also emits beta radiation, creating a massive energy source of uranium-233. It is not possible to produce nuclear bombs at any stage of this process, and it is possible to stop the reaction by stopping the proton accelerator at will. Today, Turkey's thorium reserve is capable of supplying the world's electricity for a hundred years. Dear lawmakers, Turkey's thorium studies have a depth of thirty years. Thirteen years ago, we had the opportunity to become world leaders in thorium technology. On November 20, 2007, the High Council of Science and Technology met and made the following decisions: 1) Planning the production of thorium fuel technology and proton accelerators. 2) The allocation of the budget. 3) Taking all security measures as a state policy. Only ten days after this meeting, however, a darker accident occurred on November 30, 2007 than Susuruluk. The passenger flight to Istanbul-Isparta crashed as it landed. Among those who lost their lives in the accident, the head of Turkey's thorium programme is Prof. Dr. There were five nuclear physicists in Engin Arik and his team. The plane's GPS system has been detected by the expert who knows that the black boxes aren't working, that the landing gear is malfunctioning. The accident was in WikiLeaks documents. Dear lawmakers, the EC PARTY, after this suspicious accident, almost strangely halted the thorium work in Turkey, and immediately made uranium-235-fueled nuclear power plants deals with foreign countries. Congressmans, the total amount of $18 billion that the AK PARTY has paid in the last five years as a courtship difference in companies. With this money, six thorium perfection centers could be established, and Turkey could be world leader in this area. So, what did the AK PARTY do? In 2017, he transferred the thorium fields over the Eti Mine to Turkey's Entity Fund, focusing on converting this national fortune into a ranta again. I pay my respects.

Because there's no law and justice in one man's regimes, there's no basic rights and freedoms, there's no democracy. We're looking at a very legitimate legal proposal for abuse involving indiscriminate and disproportionate provisions. While we were discussing the budget here, you rushed this proposal of the law to the Justice Commission. Why didn't you bring it before the budget? Because you did it so that the wrongs of this bill would not be discussed, so that there would be no more reactions in society. Dear lawmakers, what the Presidential Alliance called the Presidential Government system, and we called the only-man regime, were saying that those who wanted change before they were presented to the referendum were saying, "The new system will be a separation of tough forces, not to execute law proposals, not the ministers, but to discuss law proposals more, and the law changes will be discussed more; legislation will be independent and Parliament will be empowered to run. I mean, law proposals look like lawmakers on paper, but in reality, it's a law proposal to run them again. Now, that's a law proposal, so it's a law proposal that fits the essence and spirit of the one man regime. Why is that? I mean, it's just that one man has the power and the right to custody of life, I'm afraid. At the same time, in society, they fused what was good with what was needed, and they understood what was hungry.
